Đồng thời cạo sạch tàn dư silentUtterance. // ================================================================================== const MatchGame = ({ cards, t, onBack, onEndGame, onPause, mainColor, isAiMode: propAiMode, lang, onBookmark, notebook, isPaused, onLogWrong, savedState, settings, timerMode, timeLimit, initialDifficulty: propDifficulty, onAutoSave, onUpdateCards, level, onSaveToHarvest, config, globalRetryBag, addToRetryBag, removeFromRetryBag }) => { const cardsRef = React.useRef(cards); React.useEffect(() => { cardsRef.current = cards; }, [cards]); const safeShowToast = React.useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => { if (window.globalShowToast) { window.globalShowToast({ text: msg, type: type, id: Date.now() }); } }, []); const diffRef = React.useRef(propDifficulty || 'easy'); const configRef = React.useRef(config); const langRef = React.useRef(lang); const sourceModeRef = React.useRef('card'); const onSaveToHarvestRef = React.useRef(onSaveToHarvest); const onUpdateCardsRef = React.useRef(onUpdateCards); const globalRetryBagRef = React.useRef(globalRetryBag); const [matchState, setMatchState] = React.useState({ pairs: [], leftItems: [], rightItems: [], connections: {}, selectedLeft: null, isChecked: false, score: 0, correctLines: [], wrongLines: [], missingLines: [], timer: 10, isAiGenerated: false, isResultPaused: false, _isRescued: false }); const [isAiMode] = React.useState(() => { try { const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_match_config')); return cfg?.aiMode !== undefined ? cfg.aiMode : (propAiMode || false); } catch { return propAiMode || false; } }); const isAiModeRef = React.useRef(isAiMode); React.useEffect(() => { isAiModeRef.current = isAiMode; }, [isAiMode]); const [difficulty, setDifficulty] = React.useState(() => { if (savedState?.difficulty) return savedState.difficulty; try { const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_match_config')); if (cfg?.hardMode !== undefined) return cfg.hardMode ? 'hard' : 'easy'; return propDifficulty || (isAiMode ? 'hard' : 'easy'); } catch { return propDifficulty || (isAiMode ? 'hard' : 'easy'); } }); const [sourceMode] = React.useState(() => { try { const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_match_config')); return cfg?.sourceMode || 'card'; } catch { return 'card'; } }); React.useEffect(() => { diffRef.current = difficulty; }, [difficulty]); React.useEffect(() => { configRef.current = config; }, [config]); React.useEffect(() => { langRef.current = lang; }, [lang]); React.useEffect(() => { sourceModeRef.current = sourceMode; }, [sourceMode]); React.useEffect(() => { onSaveToHarvestRef.current = onSaveToHarvest; }, [onSaveToHarvest]); React.useEffect(() => { onUpdateCardsRef.current = onUpdateCards; }, [onUpdateCards]); React.useEffect(() => { globalRetryBagRef.current = globalRetryBag; }, [globalRetryBag]); const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = React.useState(timeLimit || 60); const [gameStats, setGameStats] = React.useState({ correct: 0, wrong: 0 }); const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = React.useState(false); const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = React.useState(false); const [queueLength, setQueueLength] = React.useState(0); const [aiWaitTime, setAiWaitTime] = React.useState(0); const deckRef = React.useRef([]); const coordinatorRef = React.useRef(null); const timerRef = React.useRef(null); const latestStateRef = React.useRef({}); const isGameActiveRef = React.useRef(true); const isInitializingRef = React.useRef(false); React.useEffect(() => { isGameActiveRef.current = true; return () => { isGameActiveRef.current = false; }; }, []); React.useEffect(() => { latestStateRef.current = { matchState, difficulty, gameStats, timeLeft, deck: deckRef.current, aiWaitTime }; }, [matchState, difficulty, gameStats, timeLeft, aiWaitTime]); const triggerAutoSave = React.useCallback(() => { if (isGameActiveRef.current && onAutoSave && latestStateRef.current && latestStateRef.current.matchState && latestStateRef.current.matchState.pairs.length > 0) { const currentData = latestStateRef.current; let pendingCards = []; if (coordinatorRef.current && typeof coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems === 'function') { const pendingTasks = coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems(); pendingTasks.forEach(task => { if (task && Array.isArray(task.pairs)) { pendingCards.push(...task.pairs); } else if (task && task.origin) { pendingCards.push(task.origin); } }); } const mergedDeck = [...pendingCards, ...(currentData.deck || [])]; const uniqueMergedDeck = mergedDeck.filter((v, i, a) => a.findIndex(t => (t.term === v.term)) === i); onAutoSave({ matchState: currentData.matchState, gameStats: currentData.gameStats, difficulty: currentData.difficulty, deck: uniqueMergedDeck, timeLeft: currentData.timeLeft, aiWaitTime: currentData.aiWaitTime, _needsNextOnResume: false }); } }, [onAutoSave]); React.useEffect(() => { const intervalTimer = setInterval(() => triggerAutoSave(), 5000); return () => { clearInterval(intervalTimer); triggerAutoSave(); }; }, [triggerAutoSave]); React.useEffect(() => { const queueScanner = setInterval(() => { if (coordinatorRef.current && typeof coordinatorRef.current.length === 'function') { setQueueLength(coordinatorRef.current.length()); } }, 500); return () => clearInterval(queueScanner); }, []); React.useEffect(() => { let cdTimer = null; if (aiWaitTime > 0 && !isPaused && !isGenerating && !matchState.isResultPaused) { cdTimer = setTimeout(() => setAiWaitTime(prev => prev - 1), 1000); } return () => { if (cdTimer) clearTimeout(cdTimer); }; }, [aiWaitTime, isPaused, isGenerating, matchState.isResultPaused]); const localPushToRetryBag = React.useCallback((questionData) => { if (addToRetryBag) addToRetryBag('match', questionData); }, [addToRetryBag]); const localRemoveFromRetryBag = React.useCallback((termToCheck) => { if (removeFromRetryBag) removeFromRetryBag('match', termToCheck); }, [removeFromRetryBag]); const logMistake = React.useCallback((cardOrigin) => { if (!onLogWrong || !cardOrigin) return; const mistakeCard = { ...cardOrigin, id: `mistake_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`, type: 'mistake', tags: ['match', difficulty, sourceModeRef.current], timestamp: Date.now() }; onLogWrong(mistakeCard); }, [onLogWrong, difficulty]); const fetchTask = async () => { const currentDiff = diffRef.current; const currentSourceMode = sourceModeRef.current; const currentDataMode = configRef.current?.dataMode || 'vocab'; const currentApiKey = localStorage.getItem(window.STORAGE_KEYS.API_KEY); const targetCount = currentDiff === 'hard' ? 4 : 5; let retryCards = []; if (window.Utils && window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock) { const pulledCard = window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock('match', globalRetryBagRef.current); if (pulledCard) { retryCards.push(pulledCard); } } if (currentSourceMode === 'ai_pure') { try { const meta = { id: `match_${Date.now()}`, type: 'ioe_match', level: configRef.current?.level || 'level_4_discovery_basic', topic: configRef.current?.topic || '', dataMode: currentDataMode, difficulty: currentDiff, hardMode: currentDiff === 'hard', mode: 'minigame' }; const freshHistory = window.Utils ? window.Utils.safeJsonParse('fc_ai_memory', []) : []; const qData = await window.ExamEngine.hydrateQuestion(meta, currentApiKey, freshHistory); if (qData && qData.content) { if (onSaveToHarvestRef.current && qData.content.pairs) { qData.content.pairs.forEach(c => onSaveToHarvestRef.current(meta.level, { displaySentence: c.term, targetWord: c.term, hint: c.definition })); } return qData.content; } } catch (e) { console.warn("PureMode Match Fallback:", e); if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Lỗi API/Parse Lò đúc Match AI", e); } } let realCards = []; let pool = [...retryCards]; let attempts = 0; let maxAttempts = (cardsRef.current?.length || 20) * 2; while (pool.length < targetCount && attempts < maxAttempts) { if (deckRef.current.length === 0 && currentSourceMode !== 'ai_pure') { // KHÔNG LỌC definition Ở ĐÂY NỮA, để JIT xử lý let validCards = (cardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== ''); if (validCards.length < targetCount && window.FallbackDataEngine) { const safeLang = (langRef.current === 'en' || langRef.current === 'zh') ? langRef.current : 'en'; const mode = currentDataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 20); if (fbItems && fbItems.length >= targetCount) validCards = fbItems; } if (validCards.length < targetCount) { validCards = [{ term: "Apple", definition: "Quả táo" }, { term: "Banana", definition: "Quả chuối" }, { term: "Cat", definition: "Con mèo" }, { term: "Dog", definition: "Con chó" }, { term: "Bird", definition: "Con chim" }]; } deckRef.current = window.Utils.shuffleArray(validCards); } if (deckRef.current.length > 0) { let c = deckRef.current.shift(); attempts++; // [JIT PROCESSING] Dịch thuật On-demand ngầm cho thẻ khuyết nghĩa if (c && (!c.definition || c.definition.trim() === '')) { if (isAiModeRef.current && currentApiKey && window.Utils && window.Utils.callGeminiApi) { try { const targetLang = langRef.current === 'zh' ? 'Chinese' : (langRef.current === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese' : 'English'); const prompt = `Dịch từ/câu sau sang tiếng Việt ngắn gọn nhất (dưới 6 từ). KHÔNG giải thích. Từ/câu: "${c.term}". Ngôn ngữ gốc: ${targetLang}.`; const schema = { type: "OBJECT", properties: { definition: { type: "STRING" } }, required: ["definition"] }; let dynamicTimeout = 15000; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.loadingTime) dynamicTimeout = parseInt(aiCfg.loadingTime, 10) * 1000 + 2000; } catch(e) {} const callPromise = window.Utils.callGeminiApi(prompt, schema, currentApiKey); const timeoutPromise = new Promise((_, r) => setTimeout(() => r(new Error("JIT Enrich Timeout")), dynamicTimeout)); const res = await Promise.race([callPromise, timeoutPromise]); const parsed = window.Utils.parseAiJson(res.text); if (parsed && parsed.definition) { c = { ...c, definition: parsed.definition }; if (onUpdateCardsRef.current) { const newCards = [...(cardsRef.current || [])]; const idx = newCards.findIndex(card => card.term === c.term); if (idx !== -1) { newCards[idx] = { ...newCards[idx], definition: parsed.definition }; cardsRef.current = newCards; onUpdateCardsRef.current(newCards); } } } } catch(e) { console.warn("[JIT Enrich] Lỗi dịch thuật ngầm cho từ:", c.term, e.message); } } } // Chỉ đẩy vào pool nếu thẻ đã có nghĩa (đã được JIT hoặc có sẵn) if (c && c.definition && c.definition.trim() !== '') { if (!pool.some(r => r.term === c.term)) pool.push(c); } } else { break; } } if (pool.length < targetCount) { const needed = targetCount - pool.length; if (window.FallbackDataEngine && window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData) { const safeLang = (langRef.current === 'en' || langRef.current === 'zh') ? langRef.current : 'en'; const mode = currentDataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, needed + 10); if (fbItems && fbItems.length > 0) { for (const fb of fbItems) { if (pool.length >= targetCount) break; if (!pool.some(r => r.term === fb.term)) pool.push(fb); } } } while (pool.length < targetCount) { pool.push({ term: "Fallback " + pool.length, definition: "Dự phòng " + pool.length }); } } realCards = pool.slice(0, targetCount); let trapCards = []; let isAiGenerated = false; if (currentDiff === 'hard') { const cleanDeckForTraps = (cardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== '' && c.definition && c.definition.trim() !== ''); const availableTraps = cleanDeckForTraps.filter(c => !realCards.some(r => r.term === c.term)); let trapSources = window.Utils.shuffleArray([...availableTraps]).slice(0, 2); if (trapSources.length < 2) { const needed = 2 - trapSources.length; if (window.FallbackDataEngine && window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData) { const safeLang = (langRef.current === 'en' || langRef.current === 'zh') ? langRef.current : 'en'; const mode = currentDataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 15); if (fbItems) { const validFbs = fbItems.filter(c => !realCards.some(r => r.term === c.term) && !trapSources.some(t => t.term === c.term)); trapSources = [...trapSources, ...validFbs.slice(0, needed)]; } } while (trapSources.length < 2) { trapSources.push({ term: "Trap FB " + trapSources.length, definition: "Bẫy DP " + trapSources.length }); } } for (let i = 0; i < trapSources.length; i++) { const trapCard = trapSources[i]; let distortedTerm = null; let explanation = ""; const isSentence = window.Utils.detectSentence ? window.Utils.detectSentence(trapCard.term) : trapCard.term.includes(' '); const type = isSentence ? 'sentence' : 'word'; const isAiModeActive = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_match_config'))?.aiMode || false; if (isAiModeActive && currentApiKey && window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.DistortionEngine.ai_distort) { try { const res = await window.GameAlgoEngine.DistortionEngine.ai_distort(trapCard.term, langRef.current, type, currentApiKey, configRef.current?.level, configRef.current?.topic); const returnedDistortion = type === 'word' ? res.wrongWord : res.wrongSentence; if (returnedDistortion && returnedDistortion !== trapCard.term) { distortedTerm = returnedDistortion; explanation = res.explanation; isAiGenerated = true; const currentTotal = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('fc_total_tokens') || '0', 10); localStorage.setItem('fc_total_tokens', currentTotal + 150); } } catch(e) { console.warn("AI Distortion fallback to Offline Engine", e.message); if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Lỗi API bóp méo bẫy Match", e); } } if (!distortedTerm && window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.DistortionEngine.offline_distort) { const offRes = window.GameAlgoEngine.DistortionEngine.offline_distort(trapCard.term, langRef.current, type); distortedTerm = type === 'word' ? offRes.wrongWord : offRes.wrongSentence; explanation = offRes.explanation; } else if (!distortedTerm) { distortedTerm = trapCard.term + "x"; explanation = "Lỗi hình thái từ (Offline)."; } trapCards.push({ term: distortedTerm, distortedTerm: distortedTerm, definition: trapCard.definition, explanation: explanation, originalTerm: trapCard.term, isAiGenerated: isAiGenerated }); } } const leftItems = [...realCards, ...trapCards].map((c, i) => ({ id: `left_${i}_${Date.now()}`, term: c.term, definition: c.definition || "No definition", isTrap: trapCards.some(t => t.term === c.term), origin: c })); const rightItems = []; realCards.forEach((c, i) => { rightItems.push({ id: `right_real_${i}_${Date.now()}`, term: c.term, isTrap: false }); }); for (let i = 0; i < trapCards.length; i++) { const trapCard = trapCards[i]; rightItems.push({ id: `right_trap_${i}_${Date.now()}`, term: trapCard.distortedTerm || trapCard.term, isTrap: true, originalTerm: trapCard.originalTerm, explanation: trapCard.explanation }); } const shuffleNoAdjacentTraps = (arr) => { let res = [...arr]; let valid = false; let tries = 0; while (!valid && tries < 50) { res = window.Utils.shuffleArray(res); valid = true; for (let j = 0; j < res.length - 1; j++) { if (res[j].isTrap && res[j+1].isTrap) { valid = false; break; } } tries++; } return res; }; return { pairs: realCards, leftItems: shuffleNoAdjacentTraps(leftItems), rightItems: shuffleNoAdjacentTraps(rightItems), isAiGenerated, _isRescued: false }; }; const applyMatchData = (data) => { setMatchState({ pairs: data.pairs, leftItems: data.leftItems, rightItems: data.rightItems, connections: {}, selectedLeft: null, isChecked: false, score: 0, correctLines: [], wrongLines: [], missingLines: [], timer: 10, isAiGenerated: data.isAiGenerated, isResultPaused: false, _isRescued: data._isRescued || false }); }).filter(p => p.trim() !== ""); }; if (isChinese) { parts = card.term.split(''); } else { if (isSentence) parts = card.term.split(' '); else parts = card.term.split(''); } parts = cleanAndLower(parts); window.GamePlayScreen = GamePlayScreen; // Sheet 5.6: TẦNG 5: TRÒ CHƠI HÓA (Quiz Logic) - Version 1.18 // Last Updated: 2026-06-02 18:05 // Nhiệm vụ: Tách biệt Logic, quản lý dữ liệu, API, State và Timer của Game Trắc nghiệm. // Cập nhật V1.18: [VÁ LỖI BĂNG ĐẠN ẢO] Nâng cấp queueScanner, áp dụng thuật toán đếm "đạn xịn" dựa trên cờ isAiGenerated thông qua getPendingItems(), truyền biến aiAmmoCount xuống UI để báo cáo trung thực số đạn AI. // ================================================================================== const QuizGame = ({ cards, t, mainColor, lang, timerMode, listenMode, isAiMode, timeLimit, hardMode, onPlay, onBookmark, notebook, showToast, onLogWrong, onMissingKey, isLoading, AiModeSwitch, onBack, onEndGame, savedState, settings, onAutoSave, level, onSaveToHarvest, config, globalRetryBag, addToRetryBag, removeFromRetryBag, onUpdateCards }) => { // Wrapper an toàn bảo vệ hệ thống Toast Toàn cục const safeShowToast = React.useCallback((msg, type = 'success') => { if (showToast) { showToast({ text: msg, type: type, id: Date.now() }); } }, [showToast]); const [askMode] = React.useState(() => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_quiz_config'))?.askMode || 'term'; } catch { return 'term'; } }); const [showPronun] = React.useState(() => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_quiz_config'))?.showPronun || false; } catch { return false; } }); const [autoReadAnswer] = React.useState(() => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_quiz_config'))?.autoReadAnswer || false; } catch { return false; } }); const [sourceMode] = React.useState(() => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_quiz_config'))?.sourceMode || 'card'; } catch { return 'card'; } }); const [dataMode] = React.useState(() => config?.dataMode || 'vocab'); const [gameMode, setGameMode] = React.useState(listenMode ? 'listen' : 'read'); const [currentQuestionData, setCurrentQuestionData] = React.useState(null); const [score, setScore] = React.useState(0); const [streak, setStreak] = React.useState(0); const [wrongCount, setWrongCount] = React.useState(0); const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = React.useState(timeLimit); const [isRoundStarted, setIsRoundStarted] = React.useState(!timerMode); const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = React.useState(false); const [listenPlayCount, setListenPlayCount] = React.useState(0); const [clickedWrongIndices, setClickedWrongIndices] = React.useState(new Set()); const [showChanceToast, setShowChanceToast] = React.useState(false); const [selectedOptionIndex, setSelectedOptionIndex] = React.useState(null); const [isChecking, setIsChecking] = React.useState(false); const [feedbackType, setFeedbackType] = React.useState(null); const [isAudioPlaying, setIsAudioPlaying] = React.useState(false); const [hasStartedListening, setHasStartedListening] = React.useState(false); const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = React.useState(false); const [resultTimer, setResultTimer] = React.useState(0); const [isResultPaused, setIsResultPaused] = React.useState(false); // Biến đếm đạn và Phanh UI (UI Cooldown) const [queueLength, setQueueLength] = React.useState(0); const [aiAmmoCount, setAiAmmoCount] = React.useState(0); // [NEW V1.18] Đếm đạn AI xịn const [aiWaitTime, setAiWaitTime] = React.useState(0); const audioTimeoutRef = React.useRef(null); const timerRef = React.useRef(null); const autoNextTimeoutRef = React.useRef(null); const isGameActiveRef = React.useRef(true); const coordinatorRef = React.useRef(null); const deckRef = React.useRef([]); const historyQueueRef = React.useRef([]); const cardsRef = React.useRef(cards); const stateRef = React.useRef({}); const isInitializingRef = React.useRef(false); const aiGeneratedCountRef = React.useRef(0); const configRef = React.useRef(config); // [CHUẨN HÓA ANTI-STREAK] Trí nhớ lưu vết vị trí đáp án đúng 2 lượt gần nhất const streakTrackerRef = React.useRef([]); // Đồng bộ túi nháp RAM const globalRetryBagRef = React.useRef(globalRetryBag); React.useEffect(() => { globalRetryBagRef.current = globalRetryBag; }, [globalRetryBag]); React.useEffect(() => { cardsRef.current = cards; }, [cards]); React.useEffect(() => { configRef.current = config; }, [config]); React.useEffect(() => { stateRef.current = { gameMode, score, streak, wrongCount, currentQuestionData, timeLeft, deck: deckRef.current, isAiMode, listenPlayCount, aiWaitTime }; }, [gameMode, score, streak, wrongCount, currentQuestionData, timeLeft, listenPlayCount, aiWaitTime]); // ================================================================================== // HÀM TRIGGGER AUTO SAVE (Trung tâm xử lý) // ================================================================================== const triggerAutoSave = React.useCallback(() => { if (isGameActiveRef.current && onAutoSave && stateRef.current && stateRef.current.currentQuestionData) { const currentData = stateRef.current; let pendingCards = []; if (coordinatorRef.current && typeof coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems === 'function') { const pendingTasks = coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems(); pendingCards = pendingTasks.map(task => task.origin || task.question || task).filter(Boolean); } const mergedDeck = [...pendingCards, ...(currentData.deck || [])]; const sessionData = { quizState: { currentCard: currentData.currentQuestionData?.question || null, options: currentData.currentQuestionData?.options || [], userAnswer: null, isCorrect: null, listenPlayCount: currentData.listenPlayCount, aiWaitTime: currentData.aiWaitTime }, stats: { correct: currentData.score, wrong: currentData.wrongCount }, difficulty: hardMode ? 'hard' : 'easy', deck: mergedDeck, timeLeft: currentData.timeLeft, gameMode: currentData.gameMode, streak: currentData.streak, currentQuestionData: currentData.currentQuestionData, _needsNextOnResume: false }; onAutoSave(sessionData); } }, [onAutoSave, hardMode]); React.useEffect(() => { const intervalTimer = setInterval(() => triggerAutoSave(), 5000); return () => { clearInterval(intervalTimer); triggerAutoSave(); }; }, [triggerAutoSave]); // ĐỒNG HỒ QUÉT ĐẠN NGẦM (QUEUE SCANNER 500ms) - [VÁ LỖI BĂNG ĐẠN ẢO] React.useEffect(() => { const queueScanner = setInterval(() => { if (coordinatorRef.current) { // 1. Quét tổng đạn (QueueLength truyền thống) if (typeof coordinatorRef.current.length === 'function') { setQueueLength(coordinatorRef.current.length()); } // 2. Quét đạn xịn (AI Ammo) qua hàm getPendingItems() if (typeof coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems === 'function') { const pending = coordinatorRef.current.getPendingItems(); // Chỉ đếm những viên đạn có cờ isAiGenerated là True const realAiCount = pending.filter(item => item && item.isAiGenerated).length; setAiAmmoCount(realAiCount); } } }, 500); return () => clearInterval(queueScanner); }, []); // [VÁ LỖI DEADLOCK] ĐỒNG HỒ LÀM NGUỘI UI (UI COOLDOWN TIMER) React.useEffect(() => { let cdTimer = null; if (aiWaitTime > 0 && !isPaused && !isGenerating && !isResultPaused) { cdTimer = setTimeout(() => setAiWaitTime(prev => prev - 1), 1000); } return () => { if (cdTimer) clearTimeout(cdTimer); }; }, [aiWaitTime, isPaused, isGenerating, isResultPaused]); // ================================================================================== // HÀM TIỆN ÍCH ANTI-STREAK (BẺ GÃY CHUỖI ĐÁP ÁN TRÙNG LẶP) // ================================================================================== const applyAntiStreak = (data) => { if (data && Array.isArray(data.options)) { const options = data.options; let correctIdx = options.findIndex(o => o.isCorrect === true || String(o.isCorrect).toLowerCase() === 'true'); if (correctIdx !== -1 && options.length > 1) { const streak = streakTrackerRef.current; let shuffleCount = 0; while (streak.length >= 2 && streak[0] === correctIdx && streak[1] === correctIdx && shuffleCount < 5) { let swapIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length); while (swapIdx === correctIdx) { swapIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length); } const temp = options[correctIdx]; options[correctIdx] = options[swapIdx]; options[swapIdx] = temp; correctIdx = swapIdx; shuffleCount++; console.log(`[Anti-Streak] Quiz Game: Bẻ gãy chuỗi đáp án tại vị trí mới: ${correctIdx}`); } streakTrackerRef.current = [correctIdx, ...streak].slice(0, 2); } } return data; }; // ================================================================================== // BỘ NÃO TÚI NHÁP TOÀN CỤC (GLOBAL RETRY BAG LOGIC) // ================================================================================== const localPushToRetryBag = React.useCallback((question) => { if (addToRetryBag) addToRetryBag('quiz', question); }, [addToRetryBag]); const localRemoveFromRetryBag = React.useCallback((term) => { if (removeFromRetryBag) removeFromRetryBag('quiz', term); }, [removeFromRetryBag]); const logMistake = React.useCallback((question) => { if (!onLogWrong || !question) return; const mistakeCard = { ...question, id: `mistake_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`, type: 'mistake', tags: ['quiz', hardMode ? 'hard' : 'easy', gameMode, sourceMode], timestamp: Date.now() }; onLogWrong(mistakeCard); }, [onLogWrong, hardMode, gameMode, sourceMode]); // ================================================================================== // ĐƯỜNG ỐNG DỮ LIỆU THỐNG NHẤT (UNIFIED DATA PIPELINE) - FETCH TASK // ================================================================================== const fetchTask = async () => { let card = null; const currentApiKey = localStorage.getItem(window.STORAGE_KEYS.API_KEY); if (window.Utils && window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock) { card = window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock('quiz', globalRetryBagRef.current); } if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' && !card) { if (!currentApiKey) { if (onMissingKey) onMissingKey(); console.warn("Quiz PureMode: Missing API Key, falling back to offline data."); } else { try { const meta = { id: `quiz_${Date.now()}`, type: 'quiz', level: configRef.current?.level || level || 'level_4_discovery_basic', topic: configRef.current?.topic || '', dataMode: configRef.current?.dataMode || dataMode || 'vocab', difficulty: hardMode ? 'hard' : 'easy', hardMode: hardMode, askMode: askMode, mode: 'minigame' }; const freshHistory = window.Utils ? window.Utils.safeJsonParse('fc_ai_memory', []) : []; const qData = await window.ExamEngine.hydrateQuestion(meta, currentApiKey, freshHistory); if (qData && qData.content) { if (onSaveToHarvest) { onSaveToHarvest(meta.level, { term: qData.content.question?.term, definition: qData.content.question?.definition, options: qData.content.options }, 'ioe_multiple_choice'); } return qData.content; } } catch (e) { console.warn("PureMode Forge Error:", e); if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Lỗi API/Parse Lò đúc Quiz AI", e); } } } if (!card) { if (deckRef.current.length === 0 && sourceMode !== 'ai_pure') { // [NỚI LỎNG BỘ LỌC] Chỉ yêu cầu có term, không yêu cầu definition để cho phép dịch ngầm JIT let validCards = (cardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== ''); if (validCards.length < 4 && window.FallbackDataEngine) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; const mode = configRef.current?.dataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 20); if (fbItems && fbItems.length > 0) { const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const newFbs = fbItems.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...newFbs]; } } deckRef.current = window.Utils.shuffleArray(validCards); } card = deckRef.current.shift(); } // --- BẮT ĐẦU JIT PROCESSING (DỊCH NGẦM ĐƠN LẺ) --- if (card && sourceMode === 'card' && isAiMode && currentApiKey && (!card.definition || card.definition.trim() === '')) { try { const enrichedData = await window.Utils.fillCardWithAi(card.term, lang, currentApiKey, ['definition']); if (enrichedData && enrichedData.definition) { card.definition = enrichedData.definition; // Bắn sự kiện lưu sổ tay để cập nhật vĩnh viễn if (onUpdateCards) onUpdateCards([card]); } } catch (enrichError) { console.warn("[JIT Enrich] Lỗi khi dịch ngầm thẻ:", enrichError); // Fallback nghĩa tạm thời để game không sập nếu API lỗi card.definition = card.term; } } // --- KẾT THÚC JIT PROCESSING --- if (!card) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; return window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('quiz', safeLang, configRef.current?.level || level, { isReverse: askMode === 'def' }) : { question: {term: "Fallback", definition: "Lỗi dữ liệu"}, options: [], isAiGenerated: false }; } if (historyQueueRef.current) { if (!historyQueueRef.current.includes(card.term)) { historyQueueRef.current.push(card.term); if (historyQueueRef.current.length > 400) historyQueueRef.current.shift(); try { localStorage.setItem('fc_ai_memory', JSON.stringify(historyQueueRef.current)); } catch(e) {} } } let distractors = []; let isAiGenerated = card.isAiGenerated || false; const numOptions = 4; const isReverse = askMode === 'def'; const correctOptText = isReverse ? card.term : card.definition; const correctOpt = { text: correctOptText, isCorrect: true }; const textSafe = correctOptText || ''; const isSentence = window.Utils.detectSentence ? window.Utils.detectSentence(textSafe) : textSafe.includes(' '); const type = isSentence ? 'sentence' : 'word'; try { if (hardMode && isAiMode && currentApiKey && window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.OptionsEngine.ai_generateOptions) { try { const distractorLang = isReverse ? lang : 'vi'; // [DYNAMIC TIMEOUT] Lấy cấu hình loadingTime từ AI Config, cộng thêm 5s đệm an toàn let dynamicTimeout = 25000; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.loadingTime) { dynamicTimeout = parseInt(aiCfg.loadingTime, 10) * 1000 + 5000; } } catch(e) {} // Gọi API sinh nhiễu const aiCallPromise = window.GameAlgoEngine.OptionsEngine.ai_generateOptions(card, distractorLang, type, currentApiKey, numOptions - 1, configRef.current?.level || level, configRef.current?.topic, isReverse); const timeoutPromise = new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("AI Timeout")), dynamicTimeout)); const dists = await Promise.race([aiCallPromise, timeoutPromise]); if (dists && dists.length > 0) { distractors = dists.map(d => ({ text: d, isCorrect: false })); isAiGenerated = true; const currentTotal = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('fc_total_tokens') || '0', 10); localStorage.setItem('fc_total_tokens', currentTotal + 150); } } catch (aiError) { console.warn("AI Quiz Preload Fallback:", aiError.message); } } if (distractors.length === 0 && window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.OptionsEngine.offline_generateOptions) { // Giữ bộ lọc chặt chẽ cho mảng mồi (Pool) để lấy đáp án nhiễu hợp lệ const cleanDeckForTraps = (cardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== '' && c.definition && c.definition.trim() !== ''); const distTexts = window.GameAlgoEngine.OptionsEngine.offline_generateOptions(correctOptText, cleanDeckForTraps, lang, type, numOptions - 1, isReverse); distractors = distTexts.map(d => ({ text: d, isCorrect: false })); } } catch(e) { console.warn("Fetch Options Error:", e); } if (distractors.length < numOptions - 1) { const cleanDeckForPool = (cardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== '' && c.definition && c.definition.trim() !== ''); let pool = cleanDeckForPool.filter(c => c.term !== card.term && !historyQueueRef.current.includes(c.term)); if (pool.length < numOptions - 1 && window.FallbackDataEngine) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; const mode = configRef.current?.dataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 15); if (fbItems) { const existingTerms = new Set(pool.map(p => p.term)); const newFbs = fbItems.filter(f => f.term !== card.term && !historyQueueRef.current.includes(f.term) && !existingTerms.has(f.term)); pool = [...pool, ...newFbs]; } } const needed = (numOptions - 1) - distractors.length; let randomOthers = window.Utils.shuffleArray(pool).slice(0, needed); distractors.push(...randomOthers.map(c => ({ text: isReverse ? c.term : c.definition, isCorrect: false }))); } while (distractors.length < numOptions - 1) { distractors.push({ text: correctOptText + " " + (distractors.length + 1), isCorrect: false }); } distractors = window.Utils.shuffleArray(distractors.slice(0, numOptions - 1)); const correctPos = Math.floor(Math.random() * (distractors.length + 1)); const finalOptions = [...distractors]; finalOptions.splice(correctPos, 0, correctOpt); return { question: card, options: finalOptions, isAiGenerated }; }; const handleStartGame = async () => { if (sourceMode === 'card' && (!cards || cards.length < 2)) return; // Chỉ gọi resume đánh thức AudioContext cứng của Tầng 6 if (window.fc_audioCtx && window.fc_audioCtx.state === 'suspended') { window.fc_audioCtx.resume(); } setIsGenerating(true); setListenPlayCount(0); isInitializingRef.current = true; aiGeneratedCountRef.current = 0; streakTrackerRef.current = []; // [CẮT BỎ BATCH ENRICH Ở ĐÂY] Mọi việc dịch thuật đã được giao cho JIT trong fetchTask let currentDeck = sourceMode === 'card' ? [...(cardsRef.current || [])] : []; if (sourceMode === 'card') { // Nới lỏng lưới lọc: Chỉ cần có từ vựng (term) là được vào chơi let validCards = currentDeck.filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== ''); if (validCards.length < 4) { safeShowToast("Bộ thẻ không đủ 4 thẻ hợp lệ. Kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng...", "warning"); if (window.FallbackDataEngine && window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; const mode = configRef.current?.dataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = configRef.current?.level || level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 20); if (fbItems && fbItems.length > 0) { const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const newFbs = fbItems.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...newFbs]; } } if (validCards.length < 4) { const defaults = [{ term: "Apple", definition: "Quả táo" }, { term: "Banana", definition: "Quả chuối" }, { term: "Cat", definition: "Con mèo" }, { term: "Dog", definition: "Con chó" }, { term: "Bird", definition: "Con chim" }]; const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const neededDefaults = defaults.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...neededDefaults]; } } deckRef.current = window.Utils.shuffleArray(validCards); } else { deckRef.current = []; } if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); if (window.QueueEngine && window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator) { coordinatorRef.current = window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator(fetchTask, 4); } else { safeShowToast("Lỗi hệ thống: Không tìm thấy QueueEngine (Block 2.5)", "error"); return; } const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !isAiMode; await coordinatorRef.current.maintain(true, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack); let firstData = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); if (!firstData) { if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Queue cạn đạn lúc khởi tạo Game Trắc nghiệm", "Kích hoạt Hard Fallback"); if (showToast) safeShowToast("Mạng yếu, đã kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng!", "warning"); const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; firstData = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('quiz', safeLang, configRef.current?.level || level, { isReverse: askMode === 'def' }) : null; } if (isGameActiveRef.current && firstData) { setCurrentQuestionData(applyAntiStreak(firstData)); setIsGenerating(false); isInitializingRef.current = false; let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || isAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); if (gameMode === 'listen') triggerAudio(firstData, 600); setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); } else if (isGameActiveRef.current) { safeShowToast("Lỗi nghiêm trọng khi tải dữ liệu. Buộc kết thúc.", "error"); setIsGenerating(false); isInitializingRef.current = false; onEndGame({ correct: score, wrong: wrongCount }); } }; // [DEEP DEBUG V2] Phục hồi Session const hasStartedRef = React.useRef(false); React.useEffect(() => { if (hasStartedRef.current) return; hasStartedRef.current = true; isGameActiveRef.current = true; if (savedState && savedState.currentQuestionData) { setGameMode(savedState.gameMode || (listenMode ? 'listen' : 'read')); setScore(savedState.stats?.correct || savedState.score || 0); setStreak(savedState.streak || 0); setWrongCount(savedState.stats?.wrong || savedState.wrongCount || 0); setTimeLeft(savedState.timeLeft || timeLimit); if (savedState.quizState?.listenPlayCount !== undefined) setListenPlayCount(savedState.quizState.listenPlayCount); if (savedState.quizState?.aiWaitTime !== undefined) setAiWaitTime(savedState.quizState.aiWaitTime); if (savedState.deck) { deckRef.current = [...savedState.deck]; } if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); if (window.QueueEngine && window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator) { coordinatorRef.current = window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator(fetchTask, 4); const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !isAiMode; coordinatorRef.current.maintain(false, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack); } // [BÍT LỖ HỔNG THOÁT NGANG] Xử lý cờ _needsNextOnResume if (savedState._needsNextOnResume) { setCurrentQuestionData(null); setIsGenerating(true); const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !isAiMode; coordinatorRef.current.maintain(false, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack).then(() => { if (isGameActiveRef.current) { let qData = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); if (!qData) { if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Queue cạn đạn lúc Resume Game Trắc nghiệm", "Kích hoạt Hard Fallback"); if (showToast) safeShowToast("Mạng yếu, đã kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng!", "warning"); const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; qData = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('quiz', safeLang, configRef.current?.level || level, { isReverse: askMode === 'def' }) : null; } if (qData) { setCurrentQuestionData(applyAntiStreak(qData)); setIsGenerating(false); if (savedState.gameMode === 'listen' && !timerMode) triggerAudio(qData, 600); let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || isAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); } else { safeShowToast("Lỗi tải dữ liệu. Buộc kết thúc.", "error"); setIsGenerating(false); onEndGame({ correct: savedState.stats?.correct || 0, wrong: savedState.stats?.wrong || 0 }); } } }); } else { setCurrentQuestionData(savedState.currentQuestionData); } } else { handleStartGame(); } return () => { isGameActiveRef.current = false; cleanupTimers(); window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); }; }, []); const cleanupTimers = () => { if (audioTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(audioTimeoutRef.current); if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current); if (autoNextTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(autoNextTimeoutRef.current); }; React.useEffect(() => { if (isPaused) { if (autoNextTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(autoNextTimeoutRef.current); if (audioTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(audioTimeoutRef.current); } }, [isPaused]); React.useEffect(() => { if (feedbackType && resultTimer > 0 && !isPaused && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { const id = setTimeout(() => setResultTimer(t => t - 1), 1000); return () => clearTimeout(id); } else if (feedbackType && resultTimer === 0 && !isPaused && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { handleNext(); } }, [feedbackType, resultTimer, isPaused, isResultPaused, isGenerating]); const handleSaveAndBack = () => { const currentData = stateRef.current; let needsNext = false; // [BÍT LỖ HỔNG THOÁT NGANG] Kiểm tra nếu người dùng chưa nộp bài if ((currentData.checkStatus === 'idle' || currentData.checkStatus === 'wrong_retry') && currentData.currentQuestion) { localPushToRetryBag(currentData.currentQuestion); logMistake(currentData.currentQuestion); finalWrongCount += 1; finalCombo = 1; needsNext = true; if (showToast) safeShowToast("Thoát ngang bị tính 1 lỗi sai!", "warning"); } if (onBack) { onBack({ fillBlankState: { ...currentData, wrongCount: finalWrongCount, combo: finalCombo, _needsNextOnResume: needsNext } }); } }; // ================================================================================== // ĐƯỜNG ỐNG DỮ LIỆU THỐNG NHẤT (UNIFIED DATA PIPELINE) - FETCH TASK // ================================================================================== const fetchTask = async () => { // ===================================================================== // 1. ĐÁNH CHẶN 30%: Bốc từ túi nháp (Sử dụng Động cơ Khóa Nguyên Tử Tầng 2) // Kiến trúc mới DRY: Triệt tiêu hoàn toàn Bug Bóng ma đa luồng. // ===================================================================== if (window.Utils && window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock) { const pulledCard = window.Utils.pullRetryCardWithLock('fillblank', globalRetryBagRef.current); // FillBlank đẩy toàn bộ Object đã đục lỗ vào túi nháp, nên ta return thẳng ra luôn không cần sinh lại if (pulledCard) return pulledCard; } const isPureMode = sourceMode === 'ai_pure'; const currentApiKey = localStorage.getItem(window.STORAGE_KEYS.API_KEY); // 2. AI PURE MODE: Tạo câu mới hoàn toàn từ AI thông qua ExamEngine if (isPureMode && effectiveAiMode && currentApiKey) { try { const meta = { id: `fillblank_${Date.now()}`, type: 'ioe_fill_blank', level: level || 'level_4_discovery_basic', topic: config?.topic || '', dataMode: 'sentence', mode: 'minigame' }; const freshHistory = window.Utils ? window.Utils.safeJsonParse('fc_ai_memory', []) : []; const qData = await window.ExamEngine.hydrateQuestion(meta, currentApiKey, freshHistory); if (qData && qData.content) { if (onSaveToHarvest) { onSaveToHarvest(level, { displaySentence: qData.content.displaySentence, targetWord: qData.content.targetWord, hint: qData.content.hint }, 'ioe_fill_blank'); } if (isInitializingRef.current && showToast) { safeShowToast(`Đang nạp đạn AI...`, "info"); } return qData.content; } } catch (e) { console.warn("AI FillBlank PureMode Fallback:", e.message); if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Lỗi API/Parse Máy Trộn Đề FillBlank", e); } } // 3. RÚT THẺ GỐC HOẶC TỪ KHO CỨU HỘ (Nếu Pure Mode sập hoặc đang ở Card Mode) let baseCard = null; if (!isPureMode || deckRef.current.length > 0) { if (deckRef.current.length === 0 && sourceMode !== 'ai_pure') { let validCards = (enrichedCardsRef.current || []).filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== ''); let sentenceCards = validCards.filter(c => window.Utils && window.Utils.detectSentence ? window.Utils.detectSentence(c.term) : c.term.includes(' ')); if (sentenceCards.length === 0) sentenceCards = validCards; validCards = sentenceCards; if (validCards.length < 2 && window.FallbackDataEngine) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; const mode = config?.dataMode === 'sentence' ? 'sentence' : 'vocab'; const safeLevel = config?.level || level || (safeLang === 'zh' ? 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 20); if (fbItems && fbItems.length > 0) { const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const newFbs = fbItems.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...newFbs]; } } if (validCards.length === 0) { validCards = [{ term: "Learning languages is very fun.", definition: "Học ngôn ngữ rất thú vị." }]; } deckRef.current = window.Utils.shuffleArray ? window.Utils.shuffleArray(validCards) : validCards; } baseCard = deckRef.current.shift(); } // 4. CỨU HỘ KHẨN CẤP ĐÃ ĐƯỢC CHUẨN HÓA (Gọi thẳng từ Tầng 2) if (!baseCard) { const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; const fbResult = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('fillblank', safeLang, config?.level || level, config) : null; if (fbResult) return fbResult; baseCard = { term: "Learning languages is very fun.", definition: "Học ngôn ngữ rất thú vị. (Dự phòng)" }; } // Sliding Window 400 & Sync LocalStorage cho Card Mode if (historyQueueRef.current && baseCard) { if (!historyQueueRef.current.includes(baseCard.term)) { historyQueueRef.current.push(baseCard.term); while (historyQueueRef.current.length > 400) { historyQueueRef.current.shift(); } try { localStorage.setItem('fc_ai_memory', JSON.stringify(historyQueueRef.current)); } catch(e) {} } } const isSentence = window.Utils && window.Utils.detectSentence ? window.Utils.detectSentence(baseCard.term) : baseCard.term.includes(' '); const type = isSentence ? 'sentence' : 'word'; // 5. ĐỘNG CƠ ĐỤC LỖ CHUNG (MASKING ENGINE) let maskedResult = null; if (effectiveAiMode && currentApiKey && isSentence) { try { const aiMaskPromise = window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.ai_mask(baseCard.term, baseCard.definition, lang, currentApiKey, level, config?.topic); // [VÁ LỖI CỔ CHAI] Sử dụng Dynamic Timeout thay vì Hardcode 15s let dynamicTimeout = 22000; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.loadingTime) { dynamicTimeout = parseInt(aiCfg.loadingTime, 10) * 1000 + 5000; // Cộng thêm 5s đệm mạng } } catch(e) {} const timeoutPromise = new Promise((_, r) => setTimeout(() => r(new Error("AI Mask Timeout")), dynamicTimeout)); const res = await Promise.race([aiMaskPromise, timeoutPromise]); maskedResult = { ...res, origin: baseCard, type: type, isAiGenerated: true }; } catch (e) { console.warn("AI Masking failed, fallback to offline mask:", e.message); if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Lỗi AI ClozeEngine (Đục lỗ)", e); // JIT Fallback: Ngay lập tức trả về null để luồng chính không lưu đạn lép if (deckRef.current) deckRef.current.unshift(baseCard); return null; } } // 6. CHẾ ĐỘ OFFLINE THUẦN TÚY HOẶC JIT FALLBACK NỘI BỘ if (!maskedResult) { const offRes = window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask(baseCard.term, lang, type, enrichedCardsRef.current || []); maskedResult = { ...offRes, origin: baseCard, hint: baseCard.definition || offRes.hint, type: type, isAiGenerated: false }; } return maskedResult; }; const startNewGame = async () => { setIsGenerating(true); setPlayedCount(0); setScore(0); setCombo(1); setTime(0); setCorrectCount(0); setWrongCount(0); isInitializingRef.current = true; let currentDeck = sourceMode === 'card' ? [...cards] : []; // [CẠO SẠCH BÓNG MA] Gỡ bỏ batchEnrich - Trả lại tốc độ khởi động 0s if (sourceMode === 'card') { let validCards = currentDeck.filter(c => c.term && c.term.trim() !== ''); // Nới lỏng bộ lọc: Khuyết nghĩa vẫn cho qua để dịch JIT ngầm if (validCards.length < 2) { safeShowToast("Bộ thẻ không đủ 2 thẻ hợp lệ. 'zh_level_1' : 'en_level_1'); const fbItems = window.FallbackDataEngine.getFallbackData(safeLang, mode, safeLevel, 20); if (fbItems && fbItems.length > 0) { const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const newFbs = fbItems.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...newFbs]; } } if (validCards.length < 2) { const defaults = [ { term: "Learning languages is very fun.", definition: "Học ngôn ngữ rất thú vị." }, { term: "I practice English every day.", definition: "Tôi thực hành tiếng Anh mỗi ngày." } ]; const existingTerms = new Set(validCards.map(c => c.term)); const neededDefaults = defaults.filter(f => !existingTerms.has(f.term)); validCards = [...validCards, ...neededDefaults]; } } deckRef.current = window.Utils.shuffleArray ? window.Utils.shuffleArray(validCards) : validCards; } else { deckRef.current = []; } if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); if (window.QueueEngine && window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator) { coordinatorRef.current = window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator(fetchTask, 4); } else { safeShowToast("Lỗi hệ thống: Không tìm thấy QueueEngine (Block 2.5)", "error"); return; } const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !effectiveAiMode; await coordinatorRef.current.maintain(true, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack); let data = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); // --- JUST-IN-TIME OFFLINE FALLBACK --- if (!data && effectiveAiMode && deckRef.current && deckRef.current.length > 0) { const fallbackCard = deckRef.current.shift(); if (fallbackCard) { const type = window.Utils && window.Utils.detectSentence ? (window.Utils.detectSentence(fallbackCard.term) ? 'sentence' : 'word') : 'sentence'; let offlineResult = { wrongSentence: fallbackCard.term + "x", wrongWord: fallbackCard.term + "x", correction: fallbackCard.term, explanation: "Lỗi hình thái (Dự phòng)" }; if (window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask) { offlineResult = window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask(fallbackCard.term, lang, type, enrichedCardsRef.current || []); } data = { ...offlineResult, origin: fallbackCard, isAiGenerated: false, _isRescued: true }; if (showToast) safeShowToast("AI quá tải, đã kích hoạt thuật toán nội bộ!", "warning"); } } if (!data) { if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Queue cạn đạn lúc khởi tạo Game Điền Từ", "Kích hoạt Hard Fallback"); if (showToast) safeShowToast("Mạng yếu, đã kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng!", "warning"); const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; data = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('fillblank', safeLang, config?.level || level, config) : null; } if (isGameActiveRef.current && data) { setCurrentQuestion(data); setInputBuffer(""); setCheckStatus('idle'); setAttempts(0); setIsGenerating(false); isInitializingRef.current = false; setTimeLeft(timeLimit || 30); // Kích hoạt UI Cooldown let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || effectiveAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); safeShowToast(`Hoàn tất! Buộc kết thúc.", "error"); setIsGenerating(false); isInitializingRef.current = false; if (onEndGame) onEndGame({ correct: score, wrong: wrongCount, time: time }); } if (gameTimerRef.current) clearInterval(gameTimerRef.current); gameTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => { setTime(prev => prev + 1); }, 1000); }; // PHỤC HỒI DỮ LIỆU TỪ SESSION const hasStartedRef = React.useRef(false); React.useEffect(() => { if (hasStartedRef.current) return; hasStartedRef.current = true; isGameActiveRef.current = true; if (savedState && savedState.fillBlankState) { const st = savedState.fillBlankState; setInputBuffer(st.inputBuffer || ""); setCheckStatus(st.checkStatus || 'idle'); setAttempts(st.attempts || 0); setScore(st.score || 0); setCombo(st.combo || 1); setPlayedCount(st.playedCount || 0); setTime(st.time || 0); setCorrectCount(st.correctCount || 0); setWrongCount(st.wrongCount || 0); setTimeLeft(st.timeLeft !== undefined ? st.timeLeft : (timeLimit || 30)); if (st.aiWaitTime !== undefined) setAiWaitTime(st.aiWaitTime); if (st.deck) { if (st.deck.length > 0 && typeof st.deck[0] === 'number') { deckRef.current = st.deck.map(idx => cards[idx]).filter(Boolean); } else { deckRef.current = [...st.deck]; } } if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); if (window.QueueEngine && window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator) { // ĐỒNG BỘ: Nâng mức trần nạp đạn lên 4 khi Resume Session coordinatorRef.current = window.QueueEngine.createQueueCoordinator(fetchTask, 4); const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !effectiveAiMode; coordinatorRef.current.maintain(false, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack); } // [BÍT LỖ HỔNG THOÁT NGANG] Xử lý cờ _needsNextOnResume if (st._needsNextOnResume) { setCurrentQuestion(null); setIsGenerating(true); const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !effectiveAiMode; coordinatorRef.current.maintain(false, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack).then(() => { if (isGameActiveRef.current) { let data = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); if (!data && effectiveAiMode && deckRef.current && deckRef.current.length > 0) { const fallbackCard = deckRef.current.shift(); if (fallbackCard) { const type = window.Utils && window.Utils.detectSentence ? (window.Utils.detectSentence(fallbackCard.term) ? 'sentence' : 'word') : 'sentence'; let offlineResult = { wrongSentence: fallbackCard.term + "x", wrongWord: fallbackCard.term + "x", correction: fallbackCard.term, explanation: "Lỗi hình thái (Dự phòng)" }; if (window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask) { offlineResult = window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask(fallbackCard.term, lang, type, enrichedCardsRef.current || []); } data = { ...offlineResult, origin: fallbackCard, isAiGenerated: false, _isRescued: true }; if (showToast) safeShowToast("AI quá tải, đã kích hoạt thuật toán bóp méo nội bộ!", "warning"); } } if (!data) { if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Queue cạn đạn lúc Resume Game Điền Từ", "Kích hoạt Hard Fallback"); if (showToast) safeShowToast("Mạng yếu, đã kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng!", "warning"); const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; data = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('fillblank', safeLang, config?.level || level, config) : null; } if (data) { setCurrentQuestion(data); setInputBuffer(""); setCheckStatus('idle'); setAttempts(0); setIsGenerating(false); setTimeLeft(timeLimit || 30); let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || effectiveAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); } else { safeShowToast("Lỗi tải dữ liệu. Buộc kết thúc.", "error"); setIsGenerating(false); if (onEndGame) onEndGame({ correct: st.correctCount || 0, time: st.time || 0, wrong: st.wrongCount || 0 }); } } }); } else { setCurrentQuestion(st.currentQuestion); setIsGenerating(false); isInitializingRef.current = false; } if (gameTimerRef.current) clearInterval(gameTimerRef.current); gameTimerRef.current = setInterval(() => { setTime(prev => prev + 1); }, 1000); } else { startNewGame(); } return () => { isGameActiveRef.current = false; if (gameTimerRef.current) clearInterval(gameTimerRef.current); if (coordinatorRef.current) coordinatorRef.current.destroy(); }; }, []); // [BỊT LỖ HỔNG] Thuật toán đồng bộ Phanh AI và Auto-Next const proceedToNext = (defaultDelaySec) => { if (isGameActiveRef.current) { let delaySec = defaultDelaySec; // Ép thời gian xem giải thích phải kéo dài nếu Phanh AI chưa về 0 if (aiWaitTime > delaySec) { delaySec = aiWaitTime; } if (delaySec < 1) delaySec = 1; setResultTimer(delaySec); } }; const nextRound = () => { if (isGenerating) return; setPlayedCount(prev => prev + 1); setResultTimer(0); setIsResultPaused(false); if (coordinatorRef.current && coordinatorRef.current.length() > 0) { const nextData = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); setCurrentQuestion(nextData); setInputBuffer(""); setCheckStatus('idle'); setAttempts(0); setTimeLeft(timeLimit || 30); // Kích hoạt UI Cooldown let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || effectiveAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); } else { setIsGenerating(true); const isOfflineFastTrack = sourceMode === 'card' && !effectiveAiMode; coordinatorRef.current.maintain(false, 22000, isOfflineFastTrack).then(() => { if (isGameActiveRef.current) { let data = coordinatorRef.current.pop(); if (!data && effectiveAiMode && deckRef.current && deckRef.current.length > 0) { const fallbackCard = deckRef.current.shift(); if (fallbackCard) { const type = window.Utils && window.Utils.detectSentence ? (window.Utils.detectSentence(fallbackCard.term) ? 'sentence' : 'word') : 'sentence'; let offlineResult = { wrongSentence: fallbackCard.term + "x", wrongWord: fallbackCard.term + "x", correction: fallbackCard.term, explanation: "Lỗi hình thái (Dự phòng)" }; if (window.GameAlgoEngine && window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask) { offlineResult = window.GameAlgoEngine.ClozeEngine.offline_mask(fallbackCard.term, lang, type, enrichedCardsRef.current || []); } data = { ...offlineResult, origin: fallbackCard, isAiGenerated: false, _isRescued: true }; if (showToast) safeShowToast("AI quá tải, đã kích hoạt thuật toán bóp méo nội bộ!", "warning"); } } if (!data) { if (window.logSystemEvent) window.logSystemEvent("Queue cạn đạn lúc chuyển vòng Game Điền Từ", "Kích hoạt Hard Fallback"); if (showToast) safeShowToast("Mạng yếu, đã kích hoạt dữ liệu dự phòng!", "warning"); const safeLang = (lang === 'en' || lang === 'zh') ? lang : 'en'; data = window.FallbackDataEngine ? window.FallbackDataEngine.getGameReadyFallback('fillblank', safeLang, config?.level || level, config) : null; } if (data) { setCurrentQuestion(data); setInputBuffer(""); setCheckStatus('idle'); setAttempts(0); setIsGenerating(false); setTimeLeft(timeLimit || 30); // Kích hoạt UI Cooldown let currentCooldown = 0; if (sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || effectiveAiMode) { currentCooldown = 9; try { const aiCfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fc_ai_config')); if (aiCfg && aiCfg.uiCooldown) currentCooldown = aiCfg.uiCooldown; } catch(e) {} } setAiWaitTime(currentCooldown); } else { safeShowToast("Lỗi tải dữ liệu vòng mới. Buộc kết thúc.", "error"); setIsGenerating(false); if (onEndGame) onEndGame({ correct: latestStateRef.current.correctCount, time: latestStateRef.current.time, wrong: latestStateRef.current.wrongCount }); } } }); } setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); }; const checkAnswer = () => { if (!currentQuestion || (checkStatus !== 'idle' && checkStatus !== 'wrong_retry')) return; if (inputBuffer.trim() === "") return; const normalize = (str) => { let s = str.toLowerCase(); if (window.Utils && window.Utils.advancedNormalize) s = window.Utils.advancedNormalize(s, lang || 'en'); else s = s.replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g, ''); return s.trim(); }; const cleanStr = (s) => String(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '').toLowerCase(); let isCorrect = false; const normTarget = normalize(currentQuestion.targetWord); const normInput = normalize(inputBuffer); // Lớp 1: Khớp Text thông thường (Hán tự / Tiếng Anh) if (normTarget === normInput) { isCorrect = true; } // Lớp 2: Hấp thụ Pinyin / Không dấu (Dual-Validation) else if ((lang === 'zh' || lang === 'vi') && /^[a-zA-Z\s]+$/.test(inputBuffer)) { const cleanInput = cleanStr(inputBuffer); if (cleanInput.length >= 2) { if (window.Utils && window.Utils.comparePinyin && window.Utils.comparePinyin(inputBuffer, currentQuestion.origin?.pronunciation)) { isCorrect = true; } else if (currentQuestion.origin?.pronunciation) { const cleanSentencePinyin = cleanStr(currentQuestion.origin.pronunciation); if (cleanSentencePinyin.includes(cleanInput)) { isCorrect = true; } } } } if (isCorrect) { // Phép thuật UI: Ép Input hiển thị đáp án đúng (Hấp thụ Pinyin) if (normTarget !== normInput) { setInputBuffer(currentQuestion.targetWord); } if (attempts === 0) { localRemoveFromRetryBag(currentQuestion.origin?.term || currentQuestion.targetWord); } setCheckStatus('correct'); setCorrectCount(p => p + 1); setScore(prev => prev + (15 * combo)); setCombo(prev => prev + 1); safeShowToast("Chính xác!", "success"); proceedToNext(5); } else { const newAttempts = attempts + 1; setAttempts(newAttempts); setCombo(1); setScore(prev => Math.max(0, prev - 5)); if (newAttempts >= 3) { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCheckStatus('wrong'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Đã hết lượt! Xem đáp án nhé.", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(5); } else { setCheckStatus('wrong_retry'); safeShowToast("Chưa đúng, hãy thử lại!", "warning"); setTimeout(() => setCheckStatus(p => p === 'wrong_retry' ? 'idle' : p), 1000); } } setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); }; const handleSkip = () => { if (checkStatus === 'idle' || checkStatus === 'wrong_retry') { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCombo(1); setAttempts(3); setCheckStatus('wrong'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Đã bỏ qua!", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(5); setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); } }; // ĐỘNG CƠ ĐẾM NGƯỢC THỜI GIAN React.useEffect(() => { let timerId = null; if (timerMode && (checkStatus === 'idle' || checkStatus === 'wrong_retry') && !isGenerating && currentQuestion) { timerId = setInterval(() => { setTimeLeft(prev => { if (prev <= 1) { clearInterval(timerId); return 0; } return prev - 1; }); }, 1000); } return () => { if (timerId) clearInterval(timerId); }; }, [timerMode, checkStatus, isGenerating, currentQuestion]); // CƠ CHẾ GIẬT CÒ KHI HẾT GIỜ (TIMEOUT TRIGGER) React.useEffect(() => { if (timerMode && timeLeft === 0 && (checkStatus === 'idle' || checkStatus === 'wrong_retry')) { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCombo(1); setAttempts(3); setCheckStatus('wrong'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Hết giờ! Xem đáp án nhé.", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(5); setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); } }, [timeLeft, timerMode, checkStatus, currentQuestion, logMistake, safeShowToast, triggerAutoSave, localPushToRetryBag]); // [QA DEBUG] Cải tiến useEffect đếm ngược xem kết quả, chặn Race Condition React.useEffect(() => { if ((checkStatus === 'correct' || checkStatus === 'wrong') && resultTimer > 0 && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { const id = setTimeout(() => setResultTimer(t => t - 1), 1000); return () => clearTimeout(id); } else if ((checkStatus === 'correct' || checkStatus === 'wrong') && resultTimer === 0 && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { nextRound(); } }, [checkStatus, resultTimer, isResultPaused, isGenerating]); const toggleResultPause = () => setIsResultPaused(p => !p); return { currentQuestion, inputBuffer, setInputBuffer, checkStatus, attempts, time, score, combo, playedCount, isFinished, isGenerating, aiCountdown: 0, correctCount, wrongCount, resultTimer, isResultPaused, toggleResultPause, timeLeft, aiAmmoCount, aiWaitTime, // Đã xóa queueLength checkAnswer, handleNext: nextRound, handleSkip, handleSaveAndBack }; }; // --- 2. } }; // ================================================================================== // BỘ NÃO TÚI nháp TOÀN CỤC (GLOBAL RETRY BAG LOGIC) // ================================================================================== const localPushToRetryBag = React.useCallback((questionData) => { if (addToRetryBag) addToRetryBag('spotmistake', questionData); }, [addToRetryBag]); const localRemoveFromRetryBag = React.useCallback((termToCheck) => { if (removeFromRetryBag) removeFromRetryBag('spotmistake', termToCheck); }, [removeFromRetryBag]); const logMistake = React.useCallback((question) => { if (!onLogWrong || !question) return; wordChunks = [...beforeChunks, matchChunk, ...afterChunks]; } else { wordChunks = sentence.split(' ').filter(w => w.trim().length > 0).map((text, idx) => { const cleanText = text.replace(/[.,?!;:'"]/g, '').toLowerCase(); const cleanWrong = wrong.replace(/[.,?!;:'"]/g, '').toLowerCase(); return { id: `c-${idx}-${Date.now()}`, text, isTarget: cleanText === cleanWrong }; }); } } else { wordChunks = sentence.split(' ').map((text, idx) => ({ id: `c-${idx}-${Date.now()}`, text, isTarget: false })); Hãy sửa lại cho đúng.", "success"); } else { const newAttempts = attempts + 1; setAttempts(newAttempts); setCombo(1); setScore(p => Math.max(0, p - 5)); if (newAttempts >= 3) { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCheckStatus('failed'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Đã hết lượt tìm lỗi!", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(10); } else { setCheckStatus('wrong_spot'); safeShowToast(`Chưa đúng! 'idle' : p), 1000); } } setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); }; // [NEW V1.18] LUỒNG LAZY LOADING: GỌI AI GIẢI THÍCH NGỮ PHÁP ON-DEMAND / DỊCH NGHĨA const handleRequestHint = async () => { if (isTranslating || isAiExplaining || isGenerating || !currentQuestion || !apiKey) { if (!apiKey) safeShowToast("Cần cấu hình API Key để gọi AI!", "warning"); return; } const textToTranslate = currentQuestion.origin?.term || currentQuestion.wrongSentence; if (!textToTranslate) return; setIsTranslating(true); setIsAiExplaining(true); // Dùng chung cờ Loading cho UI try { const prompt = `Dịch câu văn sau sang tiếng Việt một cách ngắn gọn, tự nhiên. CHỈ TRẢ VỀ CÂU DỊCH, KHÔNG GIẢI THÍCH THÊM: "${textToTranslate}"`; const res = await window.Utils.callGeminiApi(prompt, null, apiKey); if (res && res.text) { const translatedText = res.text.replace(/["']/g, "").trim(); // Cập nhật State để UI hiển thị ngay lập tức setCurrentQuestion(prev => ({ ...prev, hint: translatedText, origin: { ...prev.origin, definition: translatedText } })); } } catch (error) { console.warn("Lỗi dịch tự động:", error); safeShowToast("Mạng yếu hoặc lỗi AI. Không thể tải gợi ý lúc này.", "error"); } finally { setIsTranslating(false); setIsAiExplaining(false); } }; // [NEW V1.18] LUỒNG LAZY LOADING: GỌI AI GIẢI THÍCH NGỮ PHÁP ON-DEMAND const handleExplain = async () => { if (isGenerating || isAiExplaining || !currentQuestion) return; setIsAiExplaining(true); try { const contextToExplain = `Hãy giải thích ngữ pháp: Câu bị sai là "${currentQuestion.wrongSentence}". Lỗi sai ở chữ "${currentQuestion.wrongWord}" và phải sửa thành "${currentQuestion.correction}". Tại sao lại sửa như vậy?`; const result = await window.Utils.explainSentenceGrammar(contextToExplain, lang, apiKey); setCurrentQuestion(prev => ({ ...prev, explanation: result.text })); if (result._tokenUsage) { const used = result._tokenUsage.totalTokenCount || 0; const currentTotal = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('fc_total_tokens') || '0', 10); localStorage.setItem('fc_total_tokens', currentTotal + used); } } catch (e) { if (showToast) safeShowToast("Lỗi kết nối AI: " + e.message, "error"); } finally { setIsAiExplaining(false); } }; const handleCorrectionSubmit = () => { if (stage !== 'correct' || !inputBuffer.trim()) return; const normalize = (str) => str ? str.toLowerCase().replace(/[.,?!;:"'()[\]{}。，？！；：“”‘’（）【】\u0332]/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, "").trim() : ""; const normalizedInput = normalize(inputBuffer); const normalizedCorrection = normalize(currentQuestion.correction); const isChinese = /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(currentQuestion.wrongSentence || ""); const separator = isChinese ? '' : ' '; let userSentenceArray = []; const sortedIndices = [...selectedIndices].sort((a, b) => a - b); const minIdx = sortedIndices[0]; const maxIdx = sortedIndices[sortedIndices.length - 1]; for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) { if (i < minIdx || i > maxIdx) { userSentenceArray.push(chunks[i].text); } else if (i === minIdx) { userSentenceArray.push(inputBuffer); } } const userFullSentence = userSentenceArray.join(separator); const aiCorrectSentence = currentQuestion.origin?.term || currentQuestion.wrongSentence.replace(currentQuestion.wrongWord, currentQuestion.correction); const normalizedUserFull = normalize(userFullSentence); const normalizedAiFull = normalize(aiCorrectSentence); const isCorrect = (normalizedInput === normalizedCorrection) || (normalizedUserFull === normalizedAiFull); if (isCorrect) { if (attempts === 0) { localRemoveFromRetryBag(currentQuestion.origin?.term || currentQuestion.wrongSentence); } setCheckStatus('success'); setCorrectCount(p => p + 1); setScore(p => p + (20 * combo)); setCombo(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Tuyệt vời! Bạn đã sửa đúng.", "success"); proceedToNext(6); } else { const newAttempts = attempts + 1; setAttempts(newAttempts); setCombo(1); setScore(p => Math.max(0, p - 5)); if (newAttempts >= 3) { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCheckStatus('failed'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Sai quá 3 lần. Xem đáp án nhé!", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(10); } else { setCheckStatus('wrong_correct'); safeShowToast(`Sai rồi! Còn ${3 - newAttempts} lượt.`, "warning"); setTimeout(() => setCheckStatus(p => p === 'wrong_correct' ? 'correct_spot' : p), 1000); } } setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); }; const handleSkip = () => { if (checkStatus === 'success' || checkStatus === 'failed') return; localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCombo(1); setCheckStatus('failed'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Đã bỏ qua!", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(10); setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); }; // ĐỘNG CƠ ĐẾM NGƯỢC THỜI GIAN React.useEffect(() => { let timerId = null; if (timerMode && checkStatus !== 'success' && checkStatus !== 'failed' && !isGenerating && currentQuestion) { timerId = setInterval(() => { setTimeLeft(prev => { if (prev <= 1) { clearInterval(timerId); return 0; // Hết giờ } return prev - 1; }); }, 1000); } return () => { if (timerId) clearInterval(timerId); }; }, [timerMode, checkStatus, isGenerating, currentQuestion]); // CƠ CHẾ GIẬT CÒ KHI HẾT GIỜ (TIMEOUT TRIGGER) React.useEffect(() => { if (timerMode && timeLeft === 0 && checkStatus !== 'success' && checkStatus !== 'failed') { localPushToRetryBag(currentQuestion); setCombo(1); setAttempts(3); setCheckStatus('failed'); setWrongCount(p => p + 1); safeShowToast("Hết giờ! Xem đáp án nhé.", "error"); logMistake(currentQuestion); proceedToNext(10); setTimeout(() => triggerAutoSave(), 150); } }, [timeLeft, timerMode, checkStatus, currentQuestion, logMistake, safeShowToast, triggerAutoSave, localPushToRetryBag]); // [QA DEBUG] Chặn Race Condition React.useEffect(() => { if ((checkStatus === 'success' || checkStatus === 'failed') && resultTimer > 0 && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { const id = setTimeout(() => setResultTimer(t => t - 1), 1000); return () => clearTimeout(id); } else if ((checkStatus === 'success' || checkStatus === 'failed') && resultTimer === 0 && !isResultPaused && !isGenerating) { nextRound(); } }, [checkStatus, resultTimer, isResultPaused, isGenerating]); const toggleResultPause = () => setIsResultPaused(p => !p); return { currentQuestion, chunks, stage, selectedIndices, inputBuffer, setInputBuffer, checkStatus, attempts, score, combo, playedCount, time, isFinished, isGenerating, aiCountdown: 0, correctCount, wrongCount, resultTimer, isResultPaused, toggleResultPause, timeLeft, queueLength, aiWaitTime, handleSpotClick, handleCheckSpot, handleCorrectionSubmit, handleSkip, nextRound, handleSaveAndBack, onRequestExplanation: handleExplain, // Bơm hàm gọi AI giải thích ngữ pháp handleRequestHint, // Bơm hàm gọi AI dịch nghĩa isExplaining: isAiExplaining, isTranslating, isHardMode // Bơm cờ độ khó ra UI }; }; // --- 2. GAME WRAPPER --- const SpotMistakeGame = ({ cards, t, onFinish, onQuit, mainColor, lang, isAiMode, apiKey, onBookmark, notebook, onLogWrong, settings, config, onUpdateCards, onSaveToHarvest, savedState, onAutoSave, globalRetryBag, addToRetryBag, removeFromRetryBag }) => { const gameCallbacks = { onEndGame: (stats) => onFinish(stats.correct, stats.time, stats.wrong), }; const hookCallbacks = { onFinish, showToast: window.globalShowToast, onLogWrong, onUpdateCards, onSaveToHarvest, onAutoSave, onBack: onQuit, addToRetryBag, removeFromRetryBag, onEndGame: gameCallbacks.onEndGame }; const safeConfig = config || { level: 'level_4_discovery_basic', sourceMode: 'card', difficulty: 'easy' }; const effectiveAiMode = safeConfig.sourceMode === 'ai_pure' || (safeConfig.sourceMode === 'card' && safeConfig.difficulty === 'hard') || isAiMode; // [BUG FIX V1.21] Bơm ngược cờ aiMode vào cấu hình truyền xuống UI để hiển thị đúng ô đếm đạn const finalConfig = { ...safeConfig, aiMode: effectiveAiMode }; const logic = useSpotMistakeLogic(cards, { ...finalConfig, isAiMode: effectiveAiMode, apiKey, lang, timeLimit: safeConfig.timeLimit || 60, savedState, globalRetryBag }, hookCallbacks); const gameSpecificCallbacks = { onBack: logic.handleSaveAndBack, onSaveToNotebook: (payload) => { if (onBookmark) { onBookmark(payload); } else if (window.handleSaveToNotebook) { window.handleSaveToNotebook(payload); } else { if (window.globalShowToast) window.globalShowToast("Vui lòng lưu từ Sổ tay chính!", "warning"); } } }; const isBookmarked = logic.currentQuestion?.correction && notebook?.some(n => n.term.includes(logic.currentQuestion.correction)); const SpotMistakePlayScreen = window.SpotMistakePlayScreen || window.Block5_10_SpotMistakePlayScreen; if (!SpotMistakePlayScreen) return

ĐỘNG CƠ LOGIC (HOOK) --- const useOddOneOutLogic = (cards, config, callbacks) => { const { isAiMode, apiKey, lang, timeLimit, level, sourceMode, savedState, timerMode, globalRetryBag } = config; const { onFinish, showToast, onLogWrong, onUpdateCards, onSaveToHarvest, onAutoSave, onBack, addToRetryBag, removeFromRetryBag, onEndGame } = callbacks; const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = React.useState(null); const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = React.useState(null); const [checkStatus, setCheckStatus] = React.useState('idle'); const [attempts, setAttempts] = React.useState(0); const [score, setScore] = React.useState(0); const [combo, setCombo] = React.useState(1); const [playedCount, setPlayedCount] = React.useState(0); const [time, setTime] = React.useState(0); const [isFinished, setIsFinished] = React.useState(false); const [correctCount, setCorrectCount] = React.useState(0); const [wrongCount, setWrongCount] = React.useState(0); const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = React.useState(false); // UX Timer State cho màn hình hiển thị kết quả const [resultTimer, setResultTimer] = React.useState(0); const [isResultPaused, setIsResultPaused] = React.useState(false); const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = React.useState(timeLimit || 15); // Biến đếm đạn const [queueLength, setQueueLength] = React.useState(0); // [NEW V1.08] Biến Phanh UI (UI Cooldown) const [aiWaitTime, setAiWaitTime] = React.useState(0); // Động cơ thống nhất từ Tầng 2 const enrichedCardsRef = React.useRef(cards); // Khởi tạo bộ nhớ AI từ LocalStorage thay vì mảng rỗng const historyQueueRef = React.useRef(window.Utils ?
